# Continuing accounting project coordination - 2026-10-07

The user explicitly replaces the previous finite coordinator milestone with
continuing oversight throughout primary accounting implementation. This document
supersedes earlier instructions to complete coordination or pause monitoring
when R0/R1/R2 and a selected quest batch finish. Those deliveries and their
reviews remain completed checkpoints; their historical completion audit remains
valid for that batch only.

## Actual continuing Goal and finish line

The coordinator activated a new actual Goal, createdAt1791384651, status ACTIVE,
without a requested token budget. Its objective maintains both existing chats:
**Separate accounting domain preparation** (`01a11627-5fc2-7960-b5f1-f38e5183a822`)
and **Prepare quest accounting fixtures** (`01a110f7-3d22-7fc2-9965-daa6428d11fc`).
Each is to activate an actual continuing Goal with the same lifecycle, retain its
existing isolated worktree/branch and preserve all completed bundles.

The finish line is observable primary completion of the existing accounting
plan: required Plans1-5 implementation and integrated qualification, applicable
original R1-R8 requirements, supported backend/gameplay/persistence/recovery
proof, resolved required blockers, and a published owner completion disposition.
Use FINISH_ACCOUNTING_PLAN.md, REMAINING_REQUIREMENTS.md, route/coverage evidence
and exact integrated-candidate handoffs. Deployment and activation are not
authorized by this coordination Goal. Sidework delivery, an empty current queue,
quiet chats, elapsed time, or one completed review cannot satisfy this finish line.

The primary runs elsewhere. Published source and handoffs are observable; private
progress/adoption is not. A primary document reporting full completion must agree
with actual required evidence before coordinator closure. A current native build
or focused component cannot substitute for original required gameplay/recovery.

## Continuing work and ownership

After each delivery and meaningful published candidate advance, reassess actual
remaining requirements across currency/banking, item ownership, shops, auctions,
Collector, quests, crafting, gambling and paid services. Maintain an evolving
prioritized queue of concrete independent work; completed batches are checkpoints.
Do not impose another arbitrary three-extraction cap or expand merely to fill time.

Assignments specify exact operations/files/interfaces, invariants, prerequisites,
executable qualification, publication and owner dependencies. Stable owned-state
validation/calculation/effect preparation must connect to real callers. Preserve
DB/native authority, frozen identity/outcomes, native/accounting agreement,
transaction/recovery ownership, atomicity and original controls. No RAM switch,
new authority/persistence formats or generalized dual-backend framework.

The primary retains shared authorities, contracts, migrations, coverage and
activation; Plan5 retains its owner. Quest prep owns its pack/fixtures and
accepted narrow historical fixes. Architecture work must avoid its paths and
active shared-owner semantic edits. Use reservations and current preimages;
unknown private overlap stays explicit. Optional compatible bundles add no
adoption wait, release gate or rewrite of completed primary areas.

Check actual Goals/status at starts, assignment/completion boundaries and periodic
execution points. Correct drift, premature completion, idle actionable work,
research displacing implementation, weak proof and missing handoffs. Keep jobs
live across observation timeouts. Independently review exact diffs/evidence and
publish implementation/qualification/review/import dispositions and dependencies.

If independent work is temporarily exhausted, record precise unavailable
interfaces and monitor for changes that make work feasible. Do not invent tests,
repeat unchanged broad batches or silently call blocked required work complete.
Apply the actual Goal system's three-consecutive-turn blocked audit when a genuine
impasse prevents meaningful action; retain the recurring event monitor and notify
meaningful unblock evidence or needed user resumption. Pause the Goal only when
the user requests it. This does not promise uninterrupted execution in the
absence of available work or override platform Goal-state controls.

## Primary metadata repair supersedes optional patch; executable checkpoint reported - 2026-10-09

Actual published 4f45070807ac9bc86501bf81613b7b4b643bc2b9 implements the finite
BS-R1 metadata reservation repair in maintained source. Root reads both exact
diffs: reader first checks/admit outer_live_scratch+sizeof(struct stat) before
directory open/metadata inspection, then carries that metadata_live into its
checked fresh vector head/file-byte peak. Helper-directory and later file stat
lifetimes do not overlap. Lock acquisition checks two individual stat terms
before fresh strings/locking and directly includes sys/stat.h. Same-FD security,
strong outputs, caps/ABI refusal and original ownership/recovery remain intact.
Finite BS-R1 source omission is resolved; native tests/actual ABI/capacity/caller
aggregate32MiB are unqualified and remain required. No accepting gate is opened.

New reader behavior has two possible admission phases. Early callback denial
returns ENOBUFS/io_error before any genuine filesystem/ENOENT/metadata observation;
it cannot grant absence authority. If early admission succeeds, real missing file,
invalid metadata and I/O retain their own outcomes. For a valid file, later

## Broadened coordination under explicit user direction - 2026-10-08

This section supersedes any narrower reading that limits useful sidework to
immediately executable code. The user explicitly directs broader coordination.
Consider code implementation and qualification, source-grounded domain design,
acceptance/fixture blueprints, dependency reduction, integration preparation and
independent review. A missing private body or native execution prerequisite
blocks that dependent step; it does not block all useful preparation.

Every selected task names a concrete output, the future implementation effort it
removes, actual available inputs, precise missing prerequisites, owned paths and
collision limits, and the review or execution needed for its stated claim.
Reuse completed inventory/research instead of republishing summaries. Design
proposals must trace real maintained operations and preserve existing behavior;
fixture blueprints must identify authentic setup, fault cuts and assertions.
Reported private contracts can inform preparation but cannot become invented APIs
or proof of adoption, runtime acceptance or qualification. No duplicated primary
or Plan5 authority, producer, audit or backup implementation is authorized.

Maintain an evolving prioritized queue across these work types. Preserve each
live job and keep one bounded active delivery per chat. At a handoff, review its
actual output, inspect meaningful primary advances and select the next useful
independent step, including preparation when implementation must wait. Do not
manufacture documents, repeat unchanged inventories/tests or reopen passed work
to fill idle time. If no useful independent step exists, retain exact dependencies
and event monitoring. Deliveries do not change the original finish line or
native Goal status; no false resumption/completion or adoption waits follow.
